CREATE TABLE `account_security` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `two_factor_enabled` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `two_factor_method` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `password_changed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `password_reset_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `force_password_change` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `failed_login_attempts` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `last_failed_login_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `locked_until` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `security_version` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `account_security_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `account_security_user_idx` (`user_type`,`user_id`),
  KEY `account_security_locked_until_idx` (`locked_until`),
  KEY `account_security_failed_login_idx` (`last_failed_login_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=2 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `account_security_histories` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `account_security_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `user_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `event_type` varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  `event_status` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'success',
  `description` varchar(500) DEFAULT NULL,
  `failed_attempts` int(10) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `locked_until` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_type` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',
  `actor_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_agent` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `metadata` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`metadata`)),
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `account_security_histories_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `sec_history_user_idx` (`user_type`,`user_id`),
  KEY `sec_history_security_idx` (`account_security_id`),
  KEY `sec_history_event_idx` (`event_type`),
  KEY `sec_history_status_idx` (`event_status`),
  KEY `sec_history_actor_idx` (`actor_type`,`actor_id`),
  KEY `sec_history_created_idx` (`created_at`),
  KEY `sec_history_ip_idx` (`ip_address`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `accounting_accounts` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `parent_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `code` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `name` varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  `account_type` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `account_subtype` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_group` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `is_system` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `is_bank` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `is_cash` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `opening_balance` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `opening_balance_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `accounting_accounts_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  UNIQUE KEY `accounting_accounts_code_unique` (`code`),
  KEY `accounting_accounts_parent_id_foreign` (`parent_id`),
  KEY `accounting_accounts_account_type_is_active_index` (`account_type`,`is_active`),
  KEY `accounting_accounts_is_bank_is_active_index` (`is_bank`,`is_active`),
  KEY `accounting_accounts_is_cash_is_active_index` (`is_cash`,`is_active`),
  CONSTRAINT `accounting_accounts_parent_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`parent_id`) REFERENCES `accounting_accounts` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=35 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `accounting_transaction_lines` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `accounting_transaction_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `accounting_account_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `debit` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `credit` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `description` varchar(500) DEFAULT NULL,
  `reference_no` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `accounting_transaction_lines_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `acct_txn_lines_txn_account_idx` (`accounting_transaction_id`,`accounting_account_id`),
  KEY `acct_txn_lines_account_idx` (`accounting_account_id`),
  KEY `acct_txn_lines_debit_idx` (`debit`),
  KEY `acct_txn_lines_credit_idx` (`credit`),
  CONSTRAINT `accounting_transaction_lines_accounting_account_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`accounting_account_id`) REFERENCES `accounting_accounts` (`id`),
  CONSTRAINT `accounting_transaction_lines_accounting_transaction_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`accounting_transaction_id`) REFERENCES `accounting_transactions` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `accounting_transactions` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `transaction_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `transaction_date` date NOT NULL,
  `transaction_type` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `source_type` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `reference_no` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `voucher_no` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` varchar(500) DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `reversed_transaction_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `reversal_reason` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `accounting_transactions_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  UNIQUE KEY `accounting_transactions_transaction_no_unique` (`transaction_no`),
  KEY `accounting_transactions_reversed_transaction_id_foreign` (`reversed_transaction_id`),
  KEY `accounting_transactions_transaction_date_status_index` (`transaction_date`,`status`),
  KEY `accounting_transactions_transaction_type_status_index` (`transaction_type`,`status`),
  KEY `accounting_transactions_source_type_source_id_index` (`source_type`,`source_id`),
  KEY `accounting_transactions_reference_no_index` (`reference_no`),
  CONSTRAINT `accounting_transactions_reversed_transaction_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`reversed_transaction_id`) REFERENCES `accounting_transactions` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `activity_logs` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `action` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `module` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `browser` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `activity_logs_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `activity_logs_user_type_user_id_index` (`user_type`,`user_id`),
  KEY `activity_logs_module_action_index` (`module`,`action`),
  KEY `activity_logs_created_at_index` (`created_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=13 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `admins` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `admin_code` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `name` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `username` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `email` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `email_verified_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `mobile` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `password` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `password_changed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `two_factor_enabled` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `two_factor_secret` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `role` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ADMIN',
  `photo` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('active','inactive') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `last_login_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `last_login_ip` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `failed_login_attempts` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `locked_until` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `remember_token` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `admins_admin_code_unique` (`admin_code`),
  UNIQUE KEY `admins_username_unique` (`username`),
  UNIQUE KEY `admins_email_unique` (`email`),
  KEY `admins_status_role_index` (`status`,`role`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=4 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `ams_approval_steps` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `ams_request_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `revision_no` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `step_no` tinyint(3) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `approver_admin_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `comments` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `acted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `approver_member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `ams_request_revision_step_unique` (`ams_request_id`,`revision_no`,`step_no`),
  KEY `ams_approval_steps_ams_request_id_index` (`ams_request_id`),
  KEY `ams_approval_steps_approver_admin_id_index` (`approver_admin_id`),
  KEY `ams_approval_steps_status_index` (`status`),
  KEY `ams_approval_steps_approver_member_id_index` (`approver_member_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `ams_audit_logs` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `ams_request_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `actor_admin_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `action` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `from_status` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `to_status` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `comments` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `metadata` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`metadata`)),
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `revision_no` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `actor_type` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_name_snapshot` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_member_code_snapshot` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_designation_snapshot` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_agent` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `device_summary` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `location_latitude` decimal(10,7) DEFAULT NULL,
  `location_longitude` decimal(10,7) DEFAULT NULL,
  `location_accuracy_meters` decimal(10,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `location_source` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `location_captured_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `ams_audit_logs_ams_request_id_index` (`ams_request_id`),
  KEY `ams_audit_logs_actor_admin_id_index` (`actor_admin_id`),
  KEY `ams_audit_logs_actor_member_id_index` (`actor_member_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `ams_requests` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `request_no` varchar(40) NOT NULL,
  `title` varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  `request_type` varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  `reference_no` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `currency` varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BDT',
  `priority` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'normal',
  `status` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending_finance',
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `current_step` tinyint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `finance_verified_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `finance_verified_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `finance_notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `final_approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `final_approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `final_notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `attachment_path` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `attachment_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `returned_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `returned_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `return_reason` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `revision_no` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by_member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `finance_verified_by_member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `final_approved_by_member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `returned_by_member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `ams_requests_request_no_unique` (`request_no`),
  KEY `ams_requests_status_index` (`status`),
  KEY `ams_requests_created_by_index` (`created_by`),
  KEY `ams_requests_created_by_member_id_index` (`created_by_member_id`),
  KEY `ams_requests_finance_verified_by_member_id_index` (`finance_verified_by_member_id`),
  KEY `ams_requests_final_approved_by_member_id_index` (`final_approved_by_member_id`),
  KEY `ams_requests_returned_by_member_id_index` (`returned_by_member_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `ams_workflow_members` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `role` enum('trustee','finance') NOT NULL,
  `sequence` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `configured_by_admin_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `ams_workflow_members_member_id_role_unique` (`member_id`,`role`),
  UNIQUE KEY `ams_workflow_members_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `ams_workflow_members_configured_by_admin_id_foreign` (`configured_by_admin_id`),
  KEY `ams_workflow_members_role_is_active_sequence_index` (`role`,`is_active`,`sequence`),
  CONSTRAINT `ams_workflow_members_configured_by_admin_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`configured_by_admin_id`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `ams_workflow_members_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `audit_logs` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `module` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `action` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `auditable_type` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `auditable_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `old_values` longtext DEFAULT NULL,
  `new_values` longtext DEFAULT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_agent` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `request_id` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_type` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `actor_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `table_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `target_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `changed_fields` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`changed_fields`)),
  `context` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`context`)),
  `method` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `route` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `url` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `performed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `audit_logs_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `audit_logs_user_type_user_id_index` (`user_type`,`user_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `bank_accounts` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `accounting_account_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `bank_name` varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  `branch_name` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `account_name` varchar(200) NOT NULL,
  `account_number` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `account_type` varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'current',
  `routing_number` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `currency` varchar(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BDT',
  `opening_balance` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `opening_balance_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `bank_accounts_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `bank_accounts_bank_name_is_active_index` (`bank_name`,`is_active`),
  KEY `bank_accounts_account_number_index` (`account_number`),
  KEY `bank_accounts_accounting_account_id_index` (`accounting_account_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `bank_accounts_accounting_account_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`accounting_account_id`) REFERENCES `accounting_accounts` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `customers` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `customer_code` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `name` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `mobile` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `alternate_mobile` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `nid` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `customers_customer_code_unique` (`customer_code`),
  UNIQUE KEY `customers_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `customers_nid_index` (`nid`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `deposits` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `deposit_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `mr_id` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `due_amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `fine_amount` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `fine_original_amount` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `fine_waived_amount` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `fine_collected_amount` decimal(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `others_amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `advance_amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `allocation_data` longtext DEFAULT NULL,
  `financial_allocation` longtext DEFAULT NULL,
  `financial_processed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `financial_allocation_version` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `fine_applied` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `fine_applied_month` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_applied_year` smallint(6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_applied_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_applied_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_method` enum('cash','bank','bkash','nagad','rocket') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cash',
  `transaction_id` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `bank_name` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `from_bank` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `to_bank_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `deposit_date` date NOT NULL,
  `month` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `year` varchar(10) NOT NULL,
  `receipt_file` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_waived` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `fine_waived_month` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_waived_year` smallint(6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_locked_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_waived_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `fine_waived_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('pending','approved','rejected') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `rejected_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `rejection_reason` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `rejected_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `deposits_deposit_no_unique` (`deposit_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `deposits_mr_id_unique` (`mr_id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `deposits_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `deposits_approved_by_foreign` (`approved_by`),
  KEY `deposits_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `deposits_updated_by_foreign` (`updated_by`),
  KEY `deposits_rejected_by_foreign` (`rejected_by`),
  KEY `deposits_member_id_status_index` (`member_id`,`status`),
  KEY `deposits_year_month_index` (`year`,`month`),
  KEY `deposits_deposit_no_index` (`deposit_no`),
  KEY `deposits_transaction_id_index` (`transaction_id`),
  KEY `deposits_fine_waiver_lookup_idx` (`member_id`,`fine_waived`,`fine_waived_year`,`fine_waived_month`),
  KEY `deposits_to_bank_id_index` (`to_bank_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `deposits_approved_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`approved_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `deposits_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `deposits_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `deposits_rejected_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`rejected_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `deposits_to_bank_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`to_bank_id`) REFERENCES `bank_accounts` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `deposits_updated_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=3 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `dues` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `month` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `year` varchar(10) NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `fine` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `paid_amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `status` enum('pending','paid','waived') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `due_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_at` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `dues_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `dues_member_id_status_month_year_index` (`member_id`,`status`,`month`,`year`),
  KEY `dues_due_date_index` (`due_date`),
  CONSTRAINT `dues_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `dues_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `expenses` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `expense_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `category` varchar(191) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Office',
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `payment_method` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `to_bank_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `expense_date` date NOT NULL,
  `attachment` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `expenses_expense_no_unique` (`expense_no`),
  KEY `expenses_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `expenses_expense_no_index` (`expense_no`),
  KEY `expenses_category_expense_date_index` (`category`,`expense_date`),
  KEY `expenses_project_id_index` (`project_id`),
  KEY `expenses_payment_method_index` (`payment_method`),
  KEY `expenses_to_bank_id_index` (`to_bank_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `expenses_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `expenses_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `expenses_to_bank_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`to_bank_id`) REFERENCES `bank_accounts` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `fund_transactions` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `fund_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `transaction_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `type` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `category` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `balance_after` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `reference_type` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `reference_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `transaction_date` datetime NOT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `fund_transactions_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  UNIQUE KEY `fund_transactions_transaction_no_unique` (`transaction_no`),
  KEY `fund_transactions_fund_date_index` (`fund_id`,`transaction_date`),
  KEY `fund_transactions_reference_index` (`reference_type`,`reference_id`),
  KEY `fund_transactions_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  CONSTRAINT `fund_transactions_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `fund_transactions_fund_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`fund_id`) REFERENCES `funds` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `funds` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `fund_code` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `name` varchar(191) NOT NULL,

CREATE TABLE `import_batches` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `batch_no` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `module` varchar(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'members',
  `import_type` varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'excel',
  `file_name` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `file_path` varchar(500) DEFAULT NULL,
  `total_rows` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `processed_rows` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `successful_rows` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `failed_rows` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `skipped_rows` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `status` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `error_data` longtext DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `started_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `completed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `import_batches_batch_no_unique` (`batch_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `import_batches_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `import_batches_module_status_index` (`module`,`status`),
  KEY `import_batches_created_by_index` (`created_by`),
  KEY `import_batches_created_at_index` (`created_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `import_batches_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=2 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `investments` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `responsible_person` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `investment_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `investment_category` varchar(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'other',
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `investment_date` date NOT NULL,
  `reference_no` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_to` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_method` enum('cash','bank') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cash',
  `to_bank_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_approved` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `document_file` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `investments_investment_no_unique` (`investment_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `investments_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `investments_project_id_index` (`project_id`),
  KEY `investments_member_id_index` (`member_id`),
  KEY `investments_responsible_person_index` (`responsible_person`),
  KEY `investments_category_index` (`investment_category`),
  KEY `investments_date_index` (`investment_date`),
  KEY `investments_payment_method_index` (`payment_method`),
  KEY `investments_to_bank_id_index` (`to_bank_id`),
  KEY `investments_approved_by_index` (`approved_by`),
  KEY `investments_created_by_index` (`created_by`),
  KEY `investments_updated_by_index` (`updated_by`),
  CONSTRAINT `investments_approved_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`approved_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `investments_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `investments_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `investments_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `investments_responsible_person_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`responsible_person`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `investments_updated_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `login_attempts` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `login_identifier` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_agent` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `successful` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `failure_reason` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `attempted_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `guard` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL,
  `request_id` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `device_type` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `browser` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `platform` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `login_attempts_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `login_attempts_user_type_user_id_index` (`user_type`,`user_id`),
  KEY `login_attempts_ip_address_attempted_at_index` (`ip_address`,`attempted_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=12 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `members` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `member_code` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `member_type` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'REFERENCE',
  `membership_type` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'general',
  `name` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `name_bn` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `gender` enum('male','female','other') DEFAULT NULL,
  `date_of_birth` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `joining_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `father_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `mother_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `spouse_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `mobile` varchar(25) NOT NULL,
  `alternate_mobile` varchar(25) DEFAULT NULL,
  `email` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `email_verified_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `last_login_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `last_login_ip` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `failed_login_attempts` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `locked_until` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `nid` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `passport_no` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `present_address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `permanent_address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `home_district` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `password` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `password_changed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `two_factor_enabled` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `two_factor_secret` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `occupation` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `designation` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `company_name` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `monthly_income` decimal(10,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `bio` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `reference` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `bank_name` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `branch_name` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `account_name` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `account_number` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `routing_number` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `bkash_no` varchar(25) DEFAULT NULL,
  `nagad_no` varchar(25) DEFAULT NULL,
  `rocket_no` varchar(25) DEFAULT NULL,
  `photo` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `nid_front` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `nid_back` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `nid_online_copy` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `passport_copy` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `signature_image` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('pending','active','inactive','closed','resigned') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `approved` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `profile_updated_once` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `password_updated_once` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `shares` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `monthly_fee` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `advance_balance` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_deposit` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `pending_deposit` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_profit` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_profit_earned` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_withdraw` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `due_amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_fine` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `fine_rate` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `last_profit_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `remember_token` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `phone` varchar(25) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `members_member_code_unique` (`member_code`),
  UNIQUE KEY `members_mobile_unique` (`mobile`),
  UNIQUE KEY `members_email_unique` (`email`),
  UNIQUE KEY `members_nid_unique` (`nid`),
  UNIQUE KEY `members_passport_no_unique` (`passport_no`),
  KEY `members_approved_by_foreign` (`approved_by`),
  KEY `members_status_approved_membership_type_index` (`status`,`approved`,`membership_type`),
  KEY `members_member_code_index` (`member_code`),
  CONSTRAINT `members_approved_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`approved_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=55 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

{"CONSTRAINT_NAME":"money_receipts_sale_id_foreign","COLUMN_NAME":"sale_id","REFERENCED_TABLE_NAME":"sales","REFERENCED_COLUMN_NAME":"id"}

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `money_receipts` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `sale_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `customer_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `receipt_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `receipt_date` date NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `payment_method` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `transaction_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `print_count` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `last_printed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('active','cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `received_from` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `money_receipts_receipt_no_unique` (`receipt_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `money_receipts_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `money_receipts_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `money_receipts_payment_id_index` (`payment_id`),
  KEY `money_receipts_sale_id_index` (`sale_id`),
  KEY `money_receipts_customer_id_index` (`customer_id`),
  KEY `money_receipts_receipt_date_index` (`receipt_date`),
  KEY `money_receipts_status_index` (`status`),
  CONSTRAINT `money_receipts_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `money_receipts_customer_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`customer_id`) REFERENCES `customers` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `money_receipts_payment_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`payment_id`) REFERENCES `sale_payments` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `money_receipts_sale_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`sale_id`) REFERENCES `sales` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `monthly_payment_reminder_logs` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `reminder_date` date NOT NULL,
  `month_key` varchar(7) NOT NULL,
  `source` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'automatic',
  `batch_key` varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  `notification_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `fee_due` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `fine_due` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_due` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `monthly_reminder_batch_unique` (`member_id`,`batch_key`),
  KEY `monthly_payment_reminder_logs_member_id_index` (`member_id`),
  KEY `monthly_payment_reminder_logs_month_key_index` (`month_key`),
  KEY `monthly_payment_reminder_logs_reminder_date_index` (`reminder_date`),
  KEY `monthly_payment_reminder_logs_batch_key_index` (`batch_key`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `monthly_payment_reminder_settings` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `enabled` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `frequency` tinyint(3) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `reminder_dates` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`reminder_dates`)),
  `message` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `message_options` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`message_options`)),
  `timezone` varchar(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Asia/Dhaka',
  `duplicate_reset_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `last_run_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `next_run_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `notification_channel` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `message_max_length` int(10) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `active_members_only` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `approved_members_only` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `include_fee_due` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `include_premium_due` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `include_fine` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `include_others` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `duplicate_protection` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `notices` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `title` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `description` longtext NOT NULL,
  `publish_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('draft','published') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `notices_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `notices_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `notices_status_publish_date_index` (`status`,`publish_date`),
  CONSTRAINT `notices_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `notifications` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `owner_type` varchar(30) NOT NULL,
  `owner_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `owner_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `type` varchar(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'general',
  `title` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `message` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_type` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `action_url` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_read` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `read_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `priority` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'normal',
  `channel` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'app',
  `data` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`data`)),
  `expires_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `notifications_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `notifications_owner_index` (`owner_type`,`owner_id`),
  KEY `notifications_owner_read_index` (`owner_type`,`owner_id`,`is_read`),
  KEY `notifications_owner_created_index` (`owner_type`,`owner_id`,`created_at`),
  KEY `notifications_source_index` (`source_type`,`source_id`),
  KEY `notifications_type_index` (`type`),
  KEY `notifications_read_at_index` (`read_at`),
  KEY `notifications_expires_index` (`expires_at`),
  KEY `notifications_channel_index` (`channel`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `permission_audit_logs` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `permission_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `role_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `action` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `admin_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `admin_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `target_user_type` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `target_user_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `target_user_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `role_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `permission_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `old_value` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`old_value`)),
  `new_value` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`new_value`)),
  `request_id` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `permission_audit_logs_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `permission_audit_logs_user_type_user_id_index` (`user_type`,`user_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `profit_distributions` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `distribution_id` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `sale_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `deposit_amount` decimal(18,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_percentage` decimal(5,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_amount` decimal(18,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `distribution_date` date NOT NULL,
  `period` enum('MONTHLY','QUARTERLY','HALF_YEARLY','YEARLY') DEFAULT NULL,
  `total_profit` decimal(18,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_per_share` decimal(18,6) DEFAULT NULL,
  `total_shares` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `distributed_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `distributed_by_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_status` enum('pending','paid') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `paid_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `approval_status` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `accounting_transaction_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `profit_distributions_distribution_id_unique` (`distribution_id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `profit_distributions_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `profit_distributions_project_id_distribution_date_index` (`project_id`,`distribution_date`),
  KEY `mbr_payment_idx` (`member_id`,`payment_status`),
  KEY `profit_distributions_sale_id_index` (`sale_id`),
  KEY `profit_distributions_distribution_date_index` (`distribution_date`),
  KEY `profit_distributions_distribution_id_index` (`distribution_id`),
  KEY `profit_distributions_approval_status_index` (`approval_status`),
  KEY `profit_distributions_accounting_transaction_id_index` (`accounting_transaction_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `profit_distributions_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `profit_distributions_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `profit_distributions_sale_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`sale_id`) REFERENCES `sales` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `withdrawal_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL,
  `status` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `payment_method` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `bank_account_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `accounting_transaction_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `rejected_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,

  `member_notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `admin_notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `profit_withdrawals_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `profits` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_type` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `source_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `profit_date` date NOT NULL,
  `period` enum('MONTHLY','QUARTERLY','HALF_YEARLY','YEARLY') DEFAULT NULL,
  `distribution_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('pending','distributed','hold') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `distributed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `accounting_transaction_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_method` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `bank_account_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_reference` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `profits_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `profits_source_type_source_id_index` (`source_type`,`source_id`),
  KEY `profits_member_id_status_index` (`member_id`,`status`),
  KEY `profits_profit_date_index` (`profit_date`),
  KEY `profits_distribution_id_index` (`distribution_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `profits_distribution_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`distribution_id`) REFERENCES `profit_distributions` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `profits_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `project_costs` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `entry_type` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `legacy_source_table` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL,
  `legacy_source_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `responsible_person` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `investment_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `expense_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `investment_category` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `expense_category` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `entry_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `reference_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_to` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_method` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cash',
  `to_bank_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_approved` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `document_file` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `project_costs_legacy_source_unique` (`legacy_source_table`,`legacy_source_id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `project_costs_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `project_costs_project_type_deleted_idx` (`project_id`,`entry_type`,`deleted_at`),
  KEY `project_costs_type_approval_date_idx` (`entry_type`,`is_approved`,`entry_date`),
  KEY `project_costs_entry_type_index` (`entry_type`),
  KEY `project_costs_project_id_index` (`project_id`),
  KEY `project_costs_member_id_index` (`member_id`),
  KEY `project_costs_investment_no_index` (`investment_no`),
  KEY `project_costs_entry_date_index` (`entry_date`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `project_documents` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `document_name` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `document_type` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `file_path` varchar(500) NOT NULL,
  `file_extension` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `file_size` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `project_documents_project_id_index` (`project_id`),
  KEY `project_documents_document_type_index` (`document_type`),
  CONSTRAINT `project_documents_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `project_expenses` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `expense_category` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL,
  `payment_method` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `to_bank_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `expense_date` date NOT NULL,
  `reference_no` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `paid_to` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `document_file` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `project_expenses_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `project_expenses_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `project_expenses_updated_by_foreign` (`updated_by`),
  KEY `project_expenses_project_id_expense_date_index` (`project_id`,`expense_date`),
  KEY `project_expenses_expense_category_index` (`expense_category`),
  KEY `project_expenses_payment_method_index` (`payment_method`),
  KEY `project_expenses_to_bank_id_index` (`to_bank_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `project_expenses_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `project_expenses_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `project_expenses_to_bank_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`to_bank_id`) REFERENCES `bank_accounts` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `project_expenses_updated_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `plot_no` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `plot_name` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `area` decimal(15,4) NOT NULL,
  `area_unit` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `unit_rate` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_price` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `sold_area` decimal(15,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0000,
  `remaining_area` decimal(15,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0000,
  `status` enum('available','partially_sold','sold','reserved','hold') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'available',
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `project_plots_project_id_plot_no_unique` (`project_id`,`plot_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `project_plots_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `project_plots_project_id_plot_no_index` (`project_id`,`plot_no`),
  KEY `project_plots_status_index` (`status`),
  CONSTRAINT `project_plots_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `projects` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `project_no` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `project_type` enum('Land','House','Commercial','Others') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Land',
  `name` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `location` varchar(500) NOT NULL,
  `total_area` decimal(15,4) DEFAULT NULL,
  `remaining_area` decimal(15,4) DEFAULT NULL,
  `area_unit` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `registration_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `start_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `end_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('upcoming','ongoing','completed','on_hold') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'upcoming',
  `profit_approval_status` varchar(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `profit_approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_approval_notes` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_approved_sales` decimal(15,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_approved_investment` decimal(15,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_approved_expenses` decimal(15,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `profit_approved_net_profit` decimal(15,2) DEFAULT NULL,
  `seller_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `seller_mobile` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `seller_nid` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `deed_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `khatian_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `dag_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `mouza` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `registry_office` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `projects_project_no_unique` (`project_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `projects_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `projects_status_index` (`status`),
  KEY `projects_project_type_index` (`project_type`),
  KEY `projects_name_index` (`name`),
  KEY `projects_location_index` (`location`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `received_banks` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `bank_name` varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `received_banks_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `received_banks_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `received_banks_updated_by_foreign` (`updated_by`),
  KEY `received_banks_bank_name_is_active_index` (`bank_name`,`is_active`),
  CONSTRAINT `received_banks_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `received_banks_updated_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`) REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `resignations` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `resignation_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `request_date` date NOT NULL,
  `reason` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `refund_amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `status` enum('pending','approved','rejected') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `rejected_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `rejection_reason` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `note` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `share_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `share_quantity` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `resignations_resignation_no_unique` (`resignation_no`),
  KEY `resignations_approved_by_foreign` (`approved_by`),
  KEY `resignations_resignation_no_index` (`resignation_no`),
  KEY `resignations_member_id_status_index` (`member_id`,`status`),
  KEY `resignations_request_date_index` (`request_date`),
  KEY `resignations_share_id_index` (`share_id`),
  CONSTRAINT `resignations_approved_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`approved_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `resignations_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `resignations_share_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`share_id`) REFERENCES `shares` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `sale_cancellations` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `sale_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `customer_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `cancellation_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `cancellation_date` date NOT NULL,
  `sale_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `paid_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `deduction_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `refund_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `refund_status` enum('not_applicable','pending','partial','refunded') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `refund_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `refund_method` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `refund_reference` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `reason` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `approved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_cancellations_sale_id_unique` (`sale_id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_cancellations_cancellation_no_unique` (`cancellation_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_cancellations_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `sale_cancellations_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `sale_cancellations_approved_by_foreign` (`approved_by`),
  KEY `sale_cancellations_customer_id_index` (`customer_id`),
  KEY `sale_cancellations_cancellation_date_index` (`cancellation_date`),
  KEY `sale_cancellations_refund_status_index` (`refund_status`),
  CONSTRAINT `sale_cancellations_approved_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`approved_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_cancellations_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_cancellations_customer_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`customer_id`) REFERENCES `customers` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_cancellations_sale_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`sale_id`) REFERENCES `sales` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `sale_installments` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `sale_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `installment_no` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `due_date` date NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `paid_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `due_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `status` enum('pending','partial','paid','overdue','cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  `paid_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_installments_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `sale_installments_sale_id_installment_no_index` (`sale_id`,`installment_no`),
  KEY `sale_installments_due_date_index` (`due_date`),
  KEY `sale_installments_status_index` (`status`),
  CONSTRAINT `sale_installments_sale_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`sale_id`) REFERENCES `sales` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `sale_items` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `sale_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `plot_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `item_type` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'plot',
  `item_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `unit` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `quantity` decimal(15,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0000,
  `unit_rate` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_items_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `sale_items_sale_id_index` (`sale_id`),
  KEY `sale_items_project_id_index` (`project_id`),
  KEY `sale_items_plot_id_index` (`plot_id`),
  KEY `sale_items_item_type_index` (`item_type`),
  CONSTRAINT `sale_items_plot_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`plot_id`) REFERENCES `project_plots` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_items_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_items_sale_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`sale_id`) REFERENCES `sales` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `sale_payment_adjustments` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `installment_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `adjustment_date` date NOT NULL,
  `adjustment_type` enum('installment','sale_due','advance','late_fee','other') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'installment',
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_payment_adjustments_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `sale_payment_adjustments_payment_id_index` (`payment_id`),
  KEY `sale_payment_adjustments_installment_id_index` (`installment_id`),
  KEY `sale_payment_adjustments_adjustment_date_index` (`adjustment_date`),
  CONSTRAINT `sale_payment_adjustments_installment_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`installment_id`) REFERENCES `sale_installments` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_payment_adjustments_payment_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`payment_id`) REFERENCES `sale_payments` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `sale_payments` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `sale_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `customer_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `payment_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `payment_date` date NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `payment_method` enum('cash','bank','cheque','mobile_banking','card','online','other') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cash',
  `to_bank_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `bank_name` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `account_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `cheque_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `cheque_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `transaction_no` varchar(191) DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` enum('pending','approved','rejected','cancelled') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'approved',
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_payments_payment_no_unique` (`payment_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sale_payments_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `sale_payments_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `sale_payments_sale_id_index` (`sale_id`),
  KEY `sale_payments_customer_id_index` (`customer_id`),
  KEY `sale_payments_payment_date_index` (`payment_date`),
  KEY `sale_payments_payment_method_index` (`payment_method`),
  KEY `sale_payments_status_index` (`status`),
  CONSTRAINT `sale_payments_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_payments_customer_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`customer_id`) REFERENCES `customers` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sale_payments_sale_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`sale_id`) REFERENCES `sales` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `sales` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `plot_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `customer_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `sale_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `sale_type` enum('full','partial') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'full',
  `sale_unit` enum('percentage','katha','plot','bigha','acre') NOT NULL,
  `quantity` decimal(15,4) NOT NULL,
  `unit_rate` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `sale_price` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `booking_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `paid_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `due_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `has_installment` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `sale_date` date NOT NULL,
  `status` enum('active','completed','cancelled','refunded') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `purchase_price` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_expense` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `profit_amount` decimal(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sales_sale_no_unique` (`sale_no`),
  UNIQUE KEY `sales_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `sales_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `sales_updated_by_foreign` (`updated_by`),
  KEY `sales_sale_no_index` (`sale_no`),
  KEY `sales_customer_id_index` (`customer_id`),
  KEY `sales_plot_id_index` (`plot_id`),
  KEY `sales_project_id_sale_date_index` (`project_id`,`sale_date`),
  KEY `sales_status_index` (`status`),
  CONSTRAINT `sales_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sales_customer_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`customer_id`) REFERENCES `customers` (`id`),
  CONSTRAINT `sales_plot_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`plot_id`) REFERENCES `project_plots` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `sales_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `sales_updated_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `security_alerts` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `alert_type` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `severity` varchar(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'medium',
  `title` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `message` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `resolved_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `resolved_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `device_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL,
  `resolved_by_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `metadata` longtext CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_bin DEFAULT NULL CHECK (json_valid(`metadata`)),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `security_alerts_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `security_alerts_user_type_user_id_index` (`user_type`,`user_id`),
  KEY `security_alerts_severity_resolved_at_index` (`severity`,`resolved_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

CREATE TABLE `security_verifications` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `record_uuid` char(36) NOT NULL,
  `user_type` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `user_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `verification_type` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `token_hash` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `expires_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `verified_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `attempts` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `action` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `module` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `subject_type` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `subject_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `subject_uuid` char(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  `verification_method` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `device_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `status` varchar(40) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `security_verifications_record_uuid_unique` (`record_uuid`),
  KEY `security_verifications_user_type_user_id_index` (`user_type`,`user_id`),
  KEY `security_verifications_user_type_user_id_verification_type_index` (`user_type`,`user_id`,`verification_type`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

  `project_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `quantity` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `amount_per_share` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `value` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `total_amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `purchase_date` date NOT NULL,
  `status` varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `shares_share_no_unique` (`share_no`),
  KEY `shares_project_id_foreign` (`project_id`),
  KEY `shares_share_no_index` (`share_no`),
  KEY `shares_member_id_status_index` (`member_id`,`status`),
  KEY `shares_purchase_date_index` (`purchase_date`),
  CONSTRAINT `shares_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `shares_project_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`project_id`) REFERENCES `projects` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `transactions` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `transaction_no` varchar(50) NOT NULL,
  `transactionable_type` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `transactionable_id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL,
  `member_id` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_by` bigint(20) unsigned DEFAULT NULL,
  `type` enum('credit','debit') NOT NULL,
  `amount` decimal(15,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
  `payment_method` varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cash',
  `reference_no` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `transaction_date` date NOT NULL,
  `remarks` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `transactions_transaction_no_unique` (`transaction_no`),
  KEY `transactions_transactionable_type_transactionable_id_index` (`transactionable_type`,`transactionable_id`),
  KEY `transactions_member_id_foreign` (`member_id`),
  KEY `transactions_created_by_foreign` (`created_by`),
  KEY `transactions_transaction_no_index` (`transaction_no`),
  KEY `tx_morph_index` (`transactionable_type`,`transactionable_id`),
  KEY `transactions_type_transaction_date_index` (`type`,`transaction_date`),
  KEY `transactions_reference_no_index` (`reference_no`),
  CONSTRAINT `transactions_created_by_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`) REFERENCES `admins` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  CONSTRAINT `transactions_member_id_foreign` FOREIGN KEY (`member_id`) REFERENCES `members` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci

--- CREATE TABLE ---
CREATE TABLE `users` (
  `id` bigint(20) unsigned NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `email` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `email_verified_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `last_login_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `last_login_ip` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `failed_login_attempts` int(10) unsigned NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `locked_until` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `password` varchar(191) NOT NULL,
  `password_changed_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `two_factor_enabled` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `two_factor_secret` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `remember_token` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `users_email_unique` (`email`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=2 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci
